<script type="text/javascript">
function showHomepageDialog(){
	if (navigator.appName=="Microsoft Internet Explorer") {
		document.body.style.behavior='url(#default#homepage)';
		document.body.setHomePage('https://www.allmyfaves.com:443/');
	} else {
		$("#homepage_layer").dialog("open");
		$("#homepage_Iframe").attr("src","/index/makehomepage/?url=https://www.allmyfaves.com:443/");
		return false;
	}
}

function showTellafriendDialog(){
	$("#tellafriend_layer").dialog("open");
	$("#tellafriend_Iframe").attr("src","/policy/importcontacts/?nolayout");
	return false;
}

$(document).ready(function() {

				/*$('#banner').show();*/
			$('#front-wrapper').addClass('wrapper-shadow');
		
   // showlocationbar();
var  redirect_url = null;//'//www.allmyfaves.com/';
var country_name = null;

	if($.cookie('showlocation') !== 'true' && 1 == 0){
		showlocationbar();
		$(function(){
	   		var options = {path: '/', expires: 365};
	        $.cookie('showlocation', 'true', options);
		});
}

	function showlocationbar(){
	    var url = '//www.geoplugin.net/json.gp?jsoncallback=?';
	    $.getJSON(url)
            .success(function(data){
                var country_code = data.geoplugin_countryCode;
                askserverlocation(country_code);

            });
		}

function askserverlocation(country_code){
    $.getJSON('/index/getcounrtybar/?ctr='+country_code)
    .success(function(data){
	    if(data.response == 1){
		    $("#slidedown").slideDown(2000 );
		    country_name = data.counrty_name;
		    redirect_url4 = data.redirect_url;
		 //   alert(redirect_url);
		    $('#country_name').html(country_name);
		}

    });

}
var $newhr = $('<hr id="seperator"/>');
	$("#homepage_layer").dialog({
		autoOpen: false,
		modal: true,
		height: 500,
		width: 500
	});
	$("#tellafriend_layer").dialog({
		autoOpen: false,
		modal: true,
		height: 560,
		width: 730
	});

	$("#tabrenamedialog_layer").dialog({
			autoOpen: false,
			open: function() {
				$(".ui-dialog-titlebar").append($newhr);
	   	    	$('.ui-dialog-buttonpane').find('button:contains("Delete Tab")').addClass('delete-btn').css({'float':'left'});
			}


		});

		$(".ui-dialog-titlebar").append($newhr);
		$(".ui-button").addClass("blue-outer-button");

		$("#celldialogadvance_layer").dialog({
			autoOpen: false,
			open: function() {

	   	    	$('.ui-dialog-buttonpane').find('button:contains("Back")').addClass('gray-btn').css({'float':'left'});
			}


		});

		$("#dialog-confirm").next(".ui-dialog-buttonpane button").filter(function() { return $(this).text() == "Delete all items";})
		.addClass("delete-btn");
});

	function locationbar(but){
	    if(but == 1){
			 $(location).attr('href',redirect_url4);
		} else {
			$("#slidedown").hide();
	    }
	    return false;
}
</script>
